You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

---------------

   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring

   x and y are available,

• If any check fails emit form 2.

   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

---------------

   otherwise → form 2

Procedure
---------

• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

Examples
Input: 4 4 6 8
Steps:
1: 4 + 8 = 12
2: 6 - 4 = 2

---------------

---------

   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

Examples
Input: 4 4 6 8
Steps:
1: 4 + 8 = 12
2: 6 - 4 = 2
3: 2 * 12 = 24
Judge:

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

Examples
Input: 4 4 6 8
Steps:
1: 4 + 8 = 12
2: 6 - 4 = 2
3: 2 * 12 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 4 and 8 available. 4 + 8 = 12 is correct. Left 4 6 12.
Step 2: 6 and 4 available. 6 - 4 = 2 is correct. Left 12 2.
Step 3: 2 and 12 available. 2 * 12 = 24 is correct. Left 24.
Only 24 left, correct answer. 
Yes - Answer: (4 + 8) * (6 - 4) = 24

Steps:
1: 10 - 4 = 6
2: 8 / 2 = 4       
3: 4 * 6 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 10 and 4 available. 10 - 4 = 6 is correct. Left 5 10 6.
Step 2: 8 and 2 not available. Invalid. 
No

TASK
Input: 4 4 4 8
Steps:
1: 8 * 4 = 32
2: 32 - 4 = 28
3: 28 - 4 = 24
Judge:

 
 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Examples
Input: 4 5 10 10
Steps:
1: 10 - 4 = 6
2: 8 / 2 = 4       
3: 4 * 6 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 10 and 4 available. 10 - 4 = 6 is correct. Left 5 10 6. Impossible to reach 24 with 5 6 10.
Invalid at step 1 - Should be: 10 / 5 = 2

Input: 1 1 6 8
Steps:
1: 1 + 1 = 2
2: 2 + 6 = 8       
3: 8 + 8 = 16

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

Examples
Input: 4 4 6 8
Steps:
1: 4 + 8 = 12
2: 6 - 4 = 2
3: 2 * 12 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 4 and 8 available. 4 + 8 = 12 is correct. Left 4 6 12.
Step 2: 6 and 4 available. 6 - 4 = 2 is correct. Left 12 2.
Step 3: 2 and 12 available. 2 * 12 = 24 is correct. Left 24.
Only 24 left, correct answer. 
Yes - Answer: (4 + 8) * (6 - 4) = 24

No

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Invalid at step 1 - Should be: 10 / 5 = 2

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

Examples
Input: 4 4 6 8
Steps:
1: 4 + 8 = 12
2: 6 - 4 = 2
3: 2 * 12 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 4 and 8 available. 4 + 8 = 12 is correct. Left 4 6 12.
Step 2: 6 and 4 available. 6 - 4 = 2 is correct. Left 12 2.
Step 3: 2 and 12 available. 2 * 12 = 24 is correct. Left 24.
Only 24 left, correct answer. 
Yes - Answer: (4 + 8) * (6 - 4) = 24

Steps:

2: 8 / 2 = 4       
3: 4 * 6 = 24

No

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

Examples
Input: 4 4 6 8
Steps:
1: 4 + 8 = 12
2: 6 - 4 = 2
3: 2 * 12 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 4 and 8 available. 4 + 8 = 12 is correct. Left 4 6 12.
Step 2: 6 and 4 available. 6 - 4 = 2 is correct. Left 12 2.
Step 3: 2 and 12 available. 2 * 12 = 24 is correct. Left 24.
Only 24 left, correct answer. 
Yes - Answer: (4 + 8) * (6 - 4) = 24

Steps:
1: 10 - 4 = 6
2: 8 / 2 = 4       
3: 4 * 6 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 10 and 4 available. 10 - 4 = 6 is correct. Left 5 10 6.
Step 2: 8 and 2 not available. Invalid. 
No

1: 1 + 1 = 2
2: 2 + 6 = 8       
3: 8 + 8 = 16

Only 16 left, incorrect answer. Invalid.
No

No

TASK
Input: 1 3 10 11
Steps:
1: 1 * 11 = 11
2: 3 + 10 = 13
3: 11 + 13 = 24
Judge:

 
 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

Examples
Input: 4 4 6 8
Steps:
1: 4 + 8 = 12
2: 6 - 4 = 2
3: 2 * 12 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 4 and 8 available. 4 + 8 = 12 is correct. Left 4 6 12.
Step 2: 6 and 4 available. 6 - 4 = 2 is correct. Left 12 2.
Step 3: 2 and 12 available. 2 * 12 = 24 is correct. Left 24.
Only 24 left, correct answer. 
Yes - Answer: (4 + 8) * (6 - 4) = 24

Input: 4 5 10 10
Steps:
1: 10 - 4 = 6
2: 8 / 2 = 4       
3: 4 * 6 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 10 and 4 available. 10 - 4 = 6 is correct. Left 5 10 6.
Step 2: 8 and 2 not available. Invalid. 
No

Input: 1 1 6 8
Steps:
1: 1 + 1 = 2
2: 2 + 6 = 8       
3: 8 + 8 = 16
Judge:
Step 1: 1 and 1 available. 1 + 1 = 2 is correct. Left 6 8 2.
Step 2: 2 and 6 available. 2 + 6 = 8 is correct. Left 8 8.
Step 3: 8 and 8 available. 8 + 8 = 16 is correct. Left 16.
Only 16 left, incorrect answer. Invalid.
No

Input: 4 5 10 10
Steps:
1. 4 + 10 = 14    
2. 14 + 10 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 4 and 10 available. 4 + 10 = 14 is correct. Left 5 10 14.
Step 2: 14 and 10 available. 14 + 10 = 24 is correct. Left 5 24.
More than one number left. Invalid.
No

Input: 1 9 10 12
Steps:
1: 12 - 10 = 2
2: 1 + 9 = 10
3: 2 + 10 = 12
Judge:

 
 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Examples
Input: 4 5 10 10
Steps:
1: 10 - 4 = 6
2: 8 / 2 = 4       
3: 4 * 6 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 10 and 4 available. 10 - 4 = 6 is correct. Left 5 10 6. Impossible to reach 24 with 5 6 10.
Invalid at step 1 - Should be: 10 / 5 = 2

Steps:

2: 2 + 6 = 8

Step 2: 2 and 6 available. 2 + 6 = 8 is correct. Left 8 8. Impossible to reach 24 with 8 8.
Invalid at step 2 - Should be: 6 * 8 = 48

1: 12 - 10 = 2
2: 9 * 2 = 18
3: 1 + 18 = 19
Judge:

 
 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Examples
Input: 4 5 10 10
Steps:
1: 10 - 4 = 6
2: 8 / 2 = 4       
3: 4 * 6 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 10 and 4 available. 10 - 4 = 6 is correct. Left 5 10 6. Impossible to reach 24 with 5 6 10.
Invalid at step 1 - Should be: 10 / 5 = 2

Input: 1 1 6 8
Steps:
1: 1 + 1 = 2
2: 2 + 6 = 8       
3: 8 + 8 = 16
Judge:
Step 1: 1 and 1 available. 1 + 1 = 2 is correct. Left 6 8 2. Likely to reach 24 with 6 8 2.
Step 2: 2 and 6 available. 2 + 6 = 8 is correct. Left 8 8. Impossible to reach 24 with 8 8.
Invalid at step 2 - Should be: 6 * 8 = 48

Steps:
1. 4 + 10 = 14    
2. 14 + 10 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 4 and 10 available. 4 + 10 = 14 is correct. Left 5 10 14. Impossible to reach 24 with 5 10 14.
Invalid at step 1 - Should be: 10 / 5 = 2

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Goal

---------------

---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,

   otherwise → form 2

Judge:

 
 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

Examples
Input: 4 4 6 8
Steps:
1: 4 + 8 = 12
2: 6 - 4 = 2
3: 2 * 12 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 4 and 8 available. 4 + 8 = 12 is correct. Left 4 6 12.
Step 2: 6 and 4 available. 6 - 4 = 2 is correct. Left 12 2.
Step 3: 2 and 12 available. 2 * 12 = 24 is correct. Left 24.
Only 24 left, correct answer. 
Yes - Answer: (4 + 8) * (6 - 4) = 24

Input: 4 5 10 10
Steps:
1: 10 - 4 = 6
2: 8 / 2 = 4       
3: 4 * 6 = 24
Judge:
Step 1: 10 and 4 available. 10 - 4 = 6 is correct. Left 5 10 6.
Step 2: 8 and 2 not available. Invalid. 
No

Input: 1 1 6 8
Steps:
1: 1 + 1 = 2
2: 2 + 6 = 8       
3: 8 + 8 = 16
Judge:
Step 1: 1 and 1 available. 1 + 1 = 2 is correct. Left 6 8 2.
Step 2: 2 and 6 available. 2 + 6 = 8 is correct. Left 8 8.
Step 3: 8 and 8 available. 8 + 8 = 16 is correct. Left 16.
Only 16 left, incorrect answer. Invalid.
No

Steps:
1. 4 + 10 = 14    
2. 14 + 10 = 24
Judge:

Step 2: 14 and 10 available. 14 + 10 = 24 is correct. Left 5 24.
More than one number left. Invalid.
No

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

3: 2 * 12 = 24

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

2: 6 - 4 = 2
3: 2 * 12 = 24

Only 24 left, correct answer.

You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Correctness prompt: 
You are an expert verifier for the Game of 24.

Goal  
Check a multi-step attempt that should turn four numbers into **24** using only + - * /. Each number can be used once. 
Check if the numbers used in each step is available and if there are arithmetic problems. 

Required output
---------------
Think step by step. 
Return your final decision in the last line in **one** of these two forms:

1. Yes - Answer: a op b op c op d = 24  
   # all steps legal, final remaining number is 24

2. No
   # This is not a valid answer

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring 
   the step is in the form of x op y = z,
   x and y are available,
   z is the correct result of x op y (no ÷0),

• If any check fails emit form 2.  

• When all steps finish:  
   one remaining number = 24 → form 1  
   otherwise → form 2

Steps:
1: 4 + 8 = 12
2: 6 - 4 = 2
3: 2 * 12 = 24

Step 2: 6 and 4 available. 6 - 4 = 2 is correct. Left 12 2.
Step 3: 2 and 12 available. 2 * 12 = 24 is correct. Left 24.
Only 24 left, correct answer. 
Yes - Answer: (4 + 8) * (6 - 4) = 24

 suggest_prompt: 
You are a coach for the Game of 24.

Goal  
You are given an incorrect answer to a game of 24.
Detect the first step after which **no further legal moves can ever reach 24**, either due to illegal or wrong steps taken.

Required output
---------------
Think step by step (you may show your reasoning).  
Return your **final decision in the last line** in this exact form:

   Invalid at step N - Should be: x op y = z (left: …)  
      # first illegal or blocking step **and** a concrete fix

Procedure
---------
• Walk through the steps in order, ensuring after this step, it is still possible to get 24.
• For each step, check:
   - Format is exactly: x op y = z (ops allowed: + - * /; division by zero is illegal).  
   - x and y are available in the current multiset (numbers used once each).  
   - Arithmetic is correct (use tolerance 1e-6 for floats).  
• If any check fails, or after applying this step the remaining numbers can never reach 24, stop and give your suggestion.
• Your suggestion must be legal from the state **before** step N

Examples

Steps:
1: 10 - 4 = 6
2: 8 / 2 = 4       
3: 4 * 6 = 24
Judge:

Invalid at step 1 - Should be: 10 / 5 = 2
